# CLAUDE.md — PageForge V2 (CONVERTER_V2)

Operating guide for any Claude session working in this project. Read this first, then
read the two living documents it points to. Keep it accurate: when a convention, gate,
or baseline changes, update this file in the same round.

---

## 0. STANDING RULE — CORPUS REGENERATION IS OPT-IN (Chris, 2026-07-29) — NON-NEGOTIABLE

**Never regenerate the module corpus unless Chris explicitly asks for it in that message.**
The trigger is the literal code **`REGENERATE CORPUS`**:

| What Chris writes | What you regenerate |
|---|---|
| *(no code present)* | **NOTHING.** Build the change, prove it in place, stop. |
| `REGENERATE CORPUS` | **EVERYTHING** — the full 393-dir corpus (§10). |
| `REGENERATE CORPUS - <scope>` | **ONLY that scope.** Anything after the code narrows it. |

Scope is written in Chris's own words and may name a subject prefix, a template family, a
series, or a handful of modules — e.g. `REGENERATE CORPUS - OSAI modules`,
`REGENERATE CORPUS - Inquiry Templates`, `REGENERATE CORPUS - BLL200's`. Resolve it to a
module-code list (`01-Claude_Modules_/<Template>/<CODE>`), **state the resolved list and its
size before running**, and if the scope is ambiguous ASK rather than guessing wide.

**Why (Chris's reasoning, recorded so it is not re-litigated):** most PageForge changes ADD
converter-side behaviour that has no counterpart in the human-developed modules at all, so a
full 393-dir rebuild proves nothing and costs a great deal of session time. Chris — who knows
the corpus — is the judge of when a rebuild is warranted, not the converter.

**What this changes about the ship discipline.** §5 step 4 and §10 still describe HOW to
regenerate; they no longer decide WHEN. Without a regeneration:

- Do everything else in the loop — measure the class first, ship behind a data flag + env
  toggle, and prove the change on the modules you touched (a targeted convert of one or two
  affected modules is fine and is NOT a corpus regeneration).
- **Say plainly in the round's report and changelog entry that the gates were NOT re-run and
  the baselines still describe the last regenerated state.** Never imply gate coverage the
  round did not have. The `*_OFF` toggle remains the reversal guarantee.
- The changelog entry still gets written and `Config.js AppVersion` still gets bumped; the
  entry records "no regeneration (not requested)" in place of the gates block.
- If a change looks genuinely corpus-wide and risky (skeleton / footer / menu scaffold / acks
  / tag normalisation), SAY SO and recommend `REGENERATE CORPUS` — then wait to be asked.

The §10a scoped-ship machinery, the ship ledger and the full-ship cadence are all subordinate
to this rule: **no code in this repository triggers a regeneration on its own initiative.**

---

## 1. Mission

**CONVERTER_V2** (a.k.a. PageForge V2) is a **data-driven converter** that turns a Te Kura
**Writers Template (`.docx`)** + its **Media List** into the **finished HTML** for an online
learning module. The bar is exact: reproduce the **human developer's hand-built HTML** from
the **raw Writers Template + Media List alone** — no per-module hand-tuning.

We are in **Phase 1**: get the page SCAFFOLD (rows / cols / activities / sections / headings /
lists / tables / widget POSITIONS) to match the human, and build the small set of "easy"
interactive widgets. Most widget internals are intentionally left as honest placeholders for
now; comparing them to the human's built widgets would be unfair, so the gates exclude them.

---

## 2. The cardinal rule — DATA OVER CODE

Output shapes live in **`data/*.json`**, not in per-module code. A fix is a **data-described
shape** plus the minimal engine logic to apply it, derived from the human developer's
**consistent OUTPUT conventions** — never a hard-coded special case for one module.

- New behaviour goes behind a **data flag** (e.g. `Emit_Templates.json … enabled:true`) **and**
  an **env toggle** (e.g. `CELLTAG_OFF`) for A/B reversal.
- If you find yourself writing `if (moduleCode === "OSAI201")`, stop — find the general
  convention instead.

---

## 3. Start-of-session checklist

0. **§0 above — REGENERATION IS OPT-IN.** Before anything else, check whether the request
   carries the `REGENERATE CORPUS` code (and any scope after it). If it does not, no
   regeneration and no gate run happens this session, however tempting.
1. **`BUILD_CHANGELOG.md`** — top entries are the most recent rounds (57, 56, 55…). They hold
   the current state, the gate baselines, and what each env toggle/data flag does. Read the
   last 3–4 rounds to load context.
2. **`reference/Decision_Framework_Human_vs_Claude.md`** — the A / B-i / B-ii / C classification
   and the **MEASUREMENT MANDATE**.
3. **`reference/Why_Progress_Stalls_And_The_Path_Forward.md`** — why the skeleton became the
   primary measure (the strategic pivot at round 50).
4. **`reference/Human_Gold_Is_The_Per_Module_Target.md`** — LEVEL 0: for a module the human has
   already built, the human's structure is the target to reproduce (derived from raw inputs, never
   copied). Sits above doc-14 and the cascade. See §6.

The current build, current gate baselines, and the open levers are in §11–§13 below — but the
**changelog is authoritative** if they ever disagree.

---

## 4. Repository map

**CONSOLIDATED LAYOUT (2026-07-09).** There is now exactly ONE copy of the engine on disk, and it
lives inside the git repo at `pageforge-site/converter-v2/`. The dev harness `CONVERTER_V2/` stays
outside the repo and reaches that one copy through **symlinks** (`CONVERTER_V2/app`, `CONVERTER_V2/data`,
`CONVERTER_V2/CLAUDE.md`, `CONVERTER_V2/BUILD_CHANGELOG.md` are all symlinks). This means NO code path
changed: `reference/tests/` still resolves the engine at `__dirname/../../app/js` and the corpus at
`../../../01-Finalized_Modules_` exactly as before. Moving `CONVERTER_V2` into the repo was rejected —
it would have forced re-pathing 4 shell scripts, 19 Python probes and `batch_convert.cjs`.

```
FINAL_MODULE_DATA/                    ← project root, NOT a git repo
├── 01-Finalized_Modules_/<CODE>/     ← HUMAN gold HTML + the raw WT/Media List .docx + *_parsed.txt
├── 01-Claude_Modules_/<CODE>/        ← CLAUDE output HTML (regenerated by batch_convert.cjs)
├── CLAUDE.md                         → symlink to pageforge-site/converter-v2/CLAUDE.md
├── CONVERTER_V2/                     ← the DEV HARNESS, outside the repo
│   ├── app                           → symlink to ../pageforge-site/converter-v2/app  (the engine)
│   ├── data                          → symlink to ../pageforge-site/converter-v2/data
│   ├── CLAUDE.md                     → symlink   (this file)
│   ├── BUILD_CHANGELOG.md            → symlink   (AUTHORITATIVE round-by-round state, newest first)
│   ├── reference/  (incl. reference/tests/ = THE GATES + batch_convert.cjs — run gates from here)
│   └── outputs/    (the _measure_* probes, manifests, baselines/)   ← real, dev-only
└── pageforge-site/                   ← THE GIT REPO (github.com/gm-tk/gm-tk.github.io)
    └── converter-v2/
        ├── app/                      ← THE ONE COPY (index.html, js/ ×32, css/)
        ├── data/                     ← 25 files on disk; git tracks the 19 the engine loads
        ├── CLAUDE.md                 ← real file
        └── BUILD_CHANGELOG.md        ← real file
```

**IMPORTANT — the project root must be `FINAL_MODULE_DATA`**, not `CONVERTER_V2`. The engine and
every gate read `../../../01-Finalized_Modules_` and `../../../01-Claude_Modules_` as siblings of
`CONVERTER_V2`. If only `CONVERTER_V2` is mounted, nothing can find the corpus.

**RETIRED — do NOT resurrect `publish-converter-to-site.sh`** (now in
`_Backups/pre_consolidation_2026-07-09/`). It did `rm -rf $DEST/app`, which would now delete the real
engine. Publishing is just a git commit from `pageforge-site/`.

Built by `reference/tests/build_granular_registry.py`; read by `granular_consensus.py` (anchor_compare + vetting). NOT read by the engine |

The basis for the SIBLING-PRECEDENCE lookup (`granular_consensus.predecessors/precedent/adjudicate`): judge a discrepancy against the SPECIFIC previously-developed sibling FIRST, doc-14 overriding, corpus a fallback. Rebuilt with the registry. Round 180 adds a `module_meta` block (subject/template_type/phase/series/dev_order) — the substrate the cascade filters over (incl. the r181 subject×phase×template grouping). Read by the DIAGNOSTIC `granular_consensus.resolve` AND (as data) by the engine's `PrecedenceResolver.js` |
| `Precedence_Cascade.json` | round 180 (6-level @ r182) — THE AUTHORITY CASCADE definition (DATA OVER CODE): the level order (doc14 → **series+template** → subject+phase+template → subject+template → subject → corpus), each level's kind (override/sibling_chain/consensus) + `decision` (`solidify`) + `solidify_share` + `tie_defers_up` + match (equals/contains) + reliability floors, `confidence_by_level`, and `engine_inherit` (the LIVE engine flags — `enabled:false` INERT default). The consolidation levels assert a value ONLY when SOLIDLY dominant (share ≥ solidify_share AND not a tie); an ambiguous split is OMITTED and defers UP (the original style-registry rule). Vet-keyword rule: NEVER bare col-md-8/col-md-12/'width' keywords on a universal rule (substring-match false-positives — the r244 BLL244 selftest catch) — now **10** subject families (adds 14.9 BLLR + 14.10 MiW/WJ; expands Languages/Pathways/Taonga/LS/HPE) + a `_cross_cutting` block (§14.11), all as SCOPE-BOUND data-driven defaults (cohort/series/level, NEW `exclude_prefixes` — BLLR is NOT BLL, PHEFUN/HPFUN are NOT §14.8), deferred-asset red-notes, component cross-refs, registry-cross-check keys. **Round 218: every family is marked `overrides_gold:true` + `_meta.gold_override_policy`** — doc-14 rules are forward-looking and outrank the module's own human gold within scope (the ONE Level-0 exception; matching gold divergences = intentional DOC-14 OVERRIDE, never regressions; gate handling per `gate_policy`). `master_enabled:false` (inert) + per-family `active`. Mechanism `reference/tests/subject_parameters.py`. NOT read live by the engine yet (roadmapped; PrecedenceResolver consults it at cascade L1) |

## 5. The disciplined loop (follow it every round)

1. **TRIANGULATE.** For every issue/fix, show the three side-by-side (the **WT-visibility
   mandate** — never an abstract description):
   - raw WT: `../01-Finalized_Modules_/<CODE>/<CODE> Writers Template*_parsed.txt`
   - human gold: `../01-Finalized_Modules_/<CODE>/<CODE>-NN.0.html` (or `<CODE>_N.0.html`)
   - Claude: `../01-Claude_Modules_/<CODE>/<CODE>-NN.html`
2. **MEASURE the class corpus-wide BEFORE coding.** Write a probe in `outputs/` (mirror the
   existing `_measure_*.py` tools). Size the lever, find the discriminator, and prove the
   scope can't disturb cases it shouldn't touch. Do **not** read an output-format skill or
   start coding before the class is measured.
3. **IMPLEMENT** behind a data flag + env toggle. Engine logic stays general; the shape is data.
4. **PROVE GATE-SAFE** (§9) — **ONLY when Chris asked for a regeneration (§0). With no
   `REGENERATE CORPUS` code in the request, SKIP this step entirely** (prove the change on the
   modules you touched instead, and say in the report that the gates were not re-run). When he
   DID ask: every protected gate holds-or-improves, 0 stale. Via the full ship
   (`ship.sh`, the backstop) OR, for a NON-corpus-wide fix with a faithful `*_OFF` toggle, the
   **scoped ship** (§10a) — rebuild only the affected modules + prove byte-identity elsewhere + the
   exact decomposition gate proof. Run the full ship on the cadence (§10a) and for any universal change.
5. **FINALISE** (§12) — prepend a changelog round, bump the build, note the flag + toggle.

## 6. Conventions & mandates

- **LEVEL 0 — THE HUMAN GOLD IS THE PER-MODULE TARGET (Chris, debugging phase).** For any module that
  ALREADY has a human-developed build (it exists in `01-Finalized_Modules_/`), that human build's
  templated structure IS the authoritative reference for that module — the conversion's target is to
  **reproduce it element-for-element**. It sits **above** doc-14 and the whole cascade (levels 1–6 only
  DERIVE structure when there is no own-gold). **GUARDRAIL (non-negotiable):** the converter must DERIVE
  that structure from the raw Writers Template + Media List via **general, data-driven rules** — it must
  **never read or copy the gold as an input** (copying the answer key scores 100% and proves nothing;
  it violates the from-raw-inputs mission + DATA OVER CODE). "Adhere to the human's structure" =
  "reproduce it by general derivation," not "look it up." This does NOT contradict the round-178 *human
  outlier* discipline: the gold is always the target; the A/B/C framework only decides HOW to respond to
  a shortfall (a general fix vs a recorded non-derivable one-off), never WHETHER the human is the target.
  Full statement: **`reference/Human_Gold_Is_The_Per_Module_Target.md`**.
  **THE ONE RECORDED EXCEPTION (Chris, 2026-07-12 — round 218): marked doc-14 overrides.** The doc-14
  Subject Global Parameters (v2026-07-09) are FORWARD-LOOKING design-team directives the gold library
  largely PREDATES; every family in `data/Subject_Global_Parameters.json` is marked
  `overrides_gold:true`. Within its scope such a rule **outranks the module's own gold**: future
  conversions adhere to doc-14 even where the gold does not, and a stocktake/vetting divergence that
  matches a marked rule is an **INTENTIONAL divergence — verdict `DOC-14 OVERRIDE`** (`vet_issue.py`;
  `doc14_override:true` on L1 verdicts), recorded and never chased back toward the gold. Live-wiring
  rounds stay regression-proof per `_meta.gold_override_policy.gate_policy` (red-notes ship
  `class="cv2-note"` = gate-excluded by construction; structural divergences ship as NAMED DOC-14
  DELTAS in the round's changelog, gates judged net of exactly those). Doc-14's own Mode-B rule is
  preserved: a built same-series sibling still wins on pure STRUCTURE, conflict Red-Flagged visibly.

- **MEASUREMENT MANDATE.** Judge a structural change FIRST on the **skeleton SCAFFOLD match**
  (`reference/tests/_skeleton_compare.py`) and the **% structurally clean**
  (`_structural_defect_audit.py`) — NOT the text-matched WRAP/WIDTH/exact numbers (those mix
  non-derivable editorial variation with real bugs and only see ~30% text-matched elements).
- **Decision framework (A / B-i / B-ii / C).** Match the human when there's (A) a tag-link or
  (B-i/B-ii) a positional/content/output convention; (C) leave true uncatalogable editorial
  variation alone. "Derivable" explicitly **includes the human's consistent OUTPUT conventions**,
  not just the writer's tag.
- **NET-POSITIVE replaces zero-regression** for the finer text-matched buckets — a rendering
  normalisation ships on **net match-to-human + protected gates holding**, even if some matched
  cells shift. (Widget BUILDS still follow "never half-build": build cleanly or keep the
  honest placeholder.)
- **Analyse HOLISTICALLY / document-level** before declaring something "non-derivable"
  (per-tag measurement can miss a page-level convention — that's how round 45 found heading
  re-levelling and round 55 found the activity-anchor rule).
- **Never silently strip a documented instruction.** A writer note to the designer (the
  `Instruction_Cues.json` vocabulary) is **retained as a red `<p>`** after its element
  (round 43). **ROUND 219 (Chris, 2026-07-12 — the online HTML Convertor project's Change
  Ledger CL-0010 + CL-0013 folded into V2): the prefixes are now the design team's
  SOURCE-SPECIFIC scheme and every note renders red + BOLD**
  (`style="color: red; font-weight: bold;"`): **"Writers Note:"** = the writer's own
  red-text note / CS instruction (was "CS:"), **"Red Flag:"** = a converter-detected issue
  (was "RED FLAG:"), **"Note from {author}:"** = a whitelisted reviewer's Word comment,
  author + text verbatim (was "{author}:"), **"Designer/Developer To Do:"** = a deferred
  asset/URL/setup of a correctly-built pattern (NEW — emitter kind "todo", reserved for the
  doc-14 live-wiring rounds). Data `Emit_Templates.red_flag.ledger_note_scheme` +
  `Comment_Authors.render`; env `NOTESCHEME_OFF` reverts ALL notes byte-for-byte to the
  r57/r72 legacy forms; the rule set is marked `overrides_gold` in
  `Subject_Global_Parameters._universal_conventions` (the gold strips all notes — never
  chase the wording in an audit). The older folds are unchanged underneath: round 70 —
  a retained note already opening with a CS-family cue ("CS Note:", "CS:", "Creative
  Services,") folds that cue away so it never doubles (`red_flag.cs_dedupe`; env
  `CSDEDUPE_OFF`); round 71 — the DEV family ("Dev:", "Dev team –", "Developer note:") and
  addressed forms ("Note to developer:", "Note to CS:") fold too (`red_flag.addressee_fold`;
  env `ADDRFOLD_OFF`, independent). NOTE:/designer/answers:/container labels are NOT folded;
  a contentless cue is kept, never stripped to a bare prefix.

## 7. The three corpora & page-pairing nuance

- `../01-Finalized_Modules_/` — **454** module dirs (the full human library, still growing — round 253
  folded the 64-module 2026-08 intake into the registries); the
  **200** modules in `reference/tests/compare_set.txt` are the comparison set (round 197 added
  TEDC401+TEDC402; round 221 added ENGJ403 — Chris supplied its gold + Claude dirs).
- `../01-Claude_Modules_/` — **201** dirs / **2019** pages (Claude's output; what gates read).
- **Page-pairing:** the human sub-paginates `0.0, 0.1, …`; since **round 243** Claude names its
  pages the SITE LIBRARY way — **`{code}_{lesson}_{part}.html`** (`SCCH302_0_0.html` overview,
  `_2_1` a dotted/sub page; env `PAGENAME_OFF` reverts the pre-243 `-NN` dash form). The gates
  pair by **content order first**, numeric filename keys as fallback — the shared page-key idiom
  in the gate tools reads the `_L_S` form as L.S (round 243), and every legacy filename form keys
  exactly as before. The `_0_0` / `_0.0` page is the module overview.

## 8. Triangulation cheatsheet

```bash
# raw WT tags for a phrase
grep -n "PHRASE" "../01-Finalized_Modules_/<CODE>/<CODE> Writers Template*_parsed.txt"
# human heading levels in document order
grep -oE "<h[1-6][^>]*>.*</h[1-6]>" "../01-Finalized_Modules_/<CODE>/<CODE>-NN.0.html"
# Claude output for the same page
grep -n "PHRASE" "../01-Claude_Modules_/<CODE>/<CODE>-NN.html"
```
A `.docx` is a zip — unzip to inspect `word/document.xml` + `word/numbering.xml` (e.g. to read
`w:ilvl` list levels). `/tmp` wipes between sandbox calls, so unzip + inspect in one call.

_check_index_sync.cjs`** guards `app/js/_modules.json` == index.html == the app/js disk set (runs FIRST

KEEP_ATTR attribute — gold's `<div class="accordion" layout>`, html.parser value None — must score,
not crash; DETECTION: a raising pair must be COUNTED in `pairs skipped (parse error): N`, never
silently dropped). Every gate run prints the skip counter — **N must be 0**; a non-zero N means pages
are INVISIBLE to the PRIMARY gate (the pre-190 bug hid 238 pairs / ~13% for months). A bare attribute
contributes NO label token (measured: Claude ships no `layout` on paired accordions, so bare==absent
agree; gold's valued `layout=standard/speech/…` render unchanged).

**Round 185 entry-parity rail (PROTECTED):** `STUB_OEMBED=1 node --require ./_deflate_raw_polyfill.cjs
_verify_entry_parity.cjs --selftest` runs SECOND in `run_all_gates.sh` (right after index-sync). The
browser app (`App.js`) and the node harness (`batch_convert.cjs`) MUST both run their prep through the
ONE choke point **`ModuleResolver.PrepareRun`** — they had drifted (App never set `run.mtkFlag`; its
bilingual refusal predated r129) and produced DIFFERENT output for the same module (HPFUN903, the D0
parity break — see the round-185 changelog + `STOCKTAKE__Track.md`). The verifier enforces the static
entry contract (no private prep steps in either entry file), dynamic option parity on a fixture
(BLL225), and its selftest proves DETECTION. **Never add a prep step to one entry — extend PrepareRun.**
Until the mtkFlag→reoMode demotion round lands (stocktake backlog item 1), the unified semantics are the
HARNESS's (mtkFlag set) so the corpus/gate baselines are unchanged; the app's fundamentals-style page
split matches the harness, not gold, by design for now.

**Gate-safety facts to remember:**
- Heading **level** and inline tags (`<b>`/`<i>`) never change the row/col/activity **wrapper
  chain**, so `compare_structure`/`body_compare` are inert to them; the **skeleton** does see
  heading level + `<b>`/`<a>`/`<img>` nodes (so matching the human there improves it).
- The skeleton is **text-stripped**, so pure text/prefix renames (e.g. CS relabel) are invisible
  to it.
- `compare_structure.py` + `body_compare.py` **exclude** Claude's converter-added notes from the
  text match (round 219 prefixes: `"red flag"` / `"writers note"` / `"designerdeveloper to do"`
  (the folded slash) / `"note from"`, PLUS the legacy `"cs "` + bare comment-author names so a
  `NOTESCHEME_OFF` corpus still passes) — the human strips all such notes, so they have no
  counterpart. Keep that parity if you add a note prefix: ADD the new folded form, never drop
  a legacy one.
- **Round 72: ALL converter notes carry a CLASS** — `cv2-note` (CS / RED FLAG retained instructions)
  and `cv2-comment` (whitelisted Word comments). The **skeleton** (`_structural_skeleton.py`) and the
  **structural-defect audit** (`_structural_defect_audit.py`) now **SKIP** these classes (the audit's
  leak scan strips them — the writer's raw `[tag]` text inside a note was never a real content leak).
  So a note is now invisible to EVERY gate regardless of position — which is why Part B could move
  notes out of the placeholder boxes without a skeleton/defect regression. If you emit a new kind of
  note, give it one of these classes so it stays gate-neutral.
- **Round 235: NESTED `cv2-interactive` exists now** (the hand-off wrapper contains the dashed box).
  Any tool that scans `<div class="…cv2-interactive` occurrences MUST collect TOP-LEVEL balanced
  spans only (resume after each subtree — the Page Stitcher / ListsAndRuns pattern); the audit's old
  every-match scan produced overlapping spans whose reversed removal corrupted offsets (repaired
  r235 with per-module identity proof). body_compare treats the wrapper's label/arrow + in-wrapper
  cv2-notes as box furniture (not member text) and its free-block filter now carries the FULL
  documented note-prefix set — its baseline is **272** under the completed filter (old-state ==
  new-state per page, proven).

## 10. Regeneration recipe

**READ §0 FIRST — this section is HOW to regenerate, never WHEN.** Nothing here runs unless
Chris's message carried the `REGENERATE CORPUS` code; if it carried a scope, regenerate only
that scope. (The old rule — "a table/heading/list/widget change is an always-present concern →
regenerate the FULL corpus" — now describes what to RECOMMEND to Chris, not what to do
unasked.) From `CONVERTER_V2/reference/tests/`:

```bash
STUB_OEMBED=1 timeout 42 node --require ./_deflate_raw_polyfill.cjs batch_convert.cjs <CODES> --force
```

- `batch_convert.cjs` defaults to `compare_set.txt` (199 modules since round 197) if no codes are
  given; pass explicit codes to scope. `--force` overwrites. `STUB_OEMBED=1` stubs oEmbed calls.
  **Regenerating a module with NO existing Claude dir creates a FLAT dir** — pre-create the nested
  `01-Claude_Modules_/<Template>/<CODE>` first so `corpus.mdir` resolves the r128 nested layout.
- **Sandbox bash has a ~45s wall limit and `/tmp` wipes between calls.** Batch ~16–22 modules per
  call; the heavy modules (**XGF9001–9006, EXBP901/EXIP901, OSAI/data-pipeline pages, MXDB302**)
  need **smaller batches (4–9)** or they time out (rc 124). 2 parallel `batch_convert` calls are
  safe; 3+ overwhelm the sandbox.
- **`OSSC301` is the one dir NOT in `compare_set.txt`** — regen it explicitly so the corpus is
  fully fresh.
- **Then verify 0 stale (mandatory) before trusting gates** — every output must be newer than the
  source files you edited:
```bash
SRC=$(stat -c '%Y' <edited source files> | sort -rn | head -1)
find ../../../01-Claude_Modules_ -name '*.html' -printf '%T@\n' | awk -v s=$SRC '$1<s' | wc -l   # must be 0
```
(`Config.js` `AppVersion` is UI-only — not stamped in output — so editing it after a regen does
NOT make pages stale; check staleness against the OUTPUT-affecting sources only.)

### 10a. SCOPED SHIP (round 175 — rebuild ONLY the affected modules, when the change is NOT corpus-wide)

The full ship above stays the **periodic backstop**, but a fix that only fires where the WT matches a
pattern `P` does NOT need a 390-dir rebuild. Because every fix ships behind a faithful `*_OFF` toggle,
**the last-shipped corpus IS the toggle-OFF state**, so a guarded path is a *provable byte-for-byte
no-op* on every module `P` skips. Scoped ship rebuilds only the affected set and PROVES the rest is
byte-identical. Do NOT scoped-ship a **corpus-wide** change (skeleton / footer / menu scaffold / acks /
tag normalisation) — those are universal; use the full ship.

1. **Detect the complete affected set — scan ALL 390 WTs, never just `compare_set`.** The round's
   MEASUREMENT PROBE is the detector, but it must cover the whole corpus (the round-174 probe scanned
   only the 197-set and MISSED ENGC101, the module the fix was named for). Emit one code-per-line list:
   - reuse the probe → `regen_affected.cjs --from-list outputs/_affected_<class>.txt`, or
   - `regen_affected.cjs --wt-regex "<pat>"` (regex over each parsed WT — for a tag-level trigger), or
   - the gold standard: run the SHIPPED guard over every WT and keep where it fires (see
     `outputs/_detect_bracketfix.cjs` — the pattern for any future class).
   The 60% guard warns if the set looks corpus-wide → switch to the full ship.
2. **Regen the affected set (and a spot-check sample) WITH THE FIX ON** — `scoped_ship.sh … ` (no
   `--no-regen`) prints the pre-sized `_batch_plan.py --codes` lines; run each in its own call.
3. **Prove + refresh:** `./scoped_ship.sh --affected <list> --toggle <FIX_OFF> --no-regen --commit`
   runs: toggle-exists · content-hash freshness · **containment** (byte-diff changed ⊆ affected, else
   under-scoped → fall back to full ship) · **completeness spot-check** (a sample of NON-affected
   modules must be byte-identical under the fix) · the exact decomposition gate proof (`_fastloop_diff`)
   · scoped baseline + content-manifest refresh · cadence check.

**Content-hash 0-stale (defeats the mtime false alarm).** After a scoped regen the untouched modules
are older-by-mtime but byte-identical — NOT stale. `python3 _content_manifest.py fresh --affected <list>`
judges CONTENT: affected must be regenerated (mtime, scoped to them); everything else must match the
manifest (mtime ignored). `_stalecheck.sh` (mtime) remains the check for the FULL ship.

**Periodic full-ship cadence (the backstop).** `_ship_ledger.py` counts scoped ships; run the full
`ship.sh` at least every **8** (hard stop **16**), and always for a corpus-wide change. `ship.sh` records
the full ship + refreshes the content manifest; `scoped_ship.sh` increments the counter and warns when due.

---

## 11. Env toggles (A/B reversal — every fix is reversible)

Set the env var when running `batch_convert.cjs` to revert that fix to its pre-round baseline.

| Toggle | Round | Reverts |
|---|---|---|
| `RELEVEL_OFF` | 45 | document-level heading re-levelling (raw writer digit + body_shift) |
| `LTABLE_OFF` | 46 | layout-table → recursive-cell `row>col` grid |
| `NAMEDH_OFF` | 47 | named learning-design heading promotion (WALT/WILF/…) |
| `ROWGROUP_OFF` | 51 | row grouping (media/table/widget flow into the section row) |
| `CELLTAG_OFF` | 54 | data-table cell-tag inline rendering (`[H2] x`→`<b>x</b>` in kept tables) |
| `RELEVELACT_OFF` | 55 | activity-anchor exclusion from the re-level pool |
| `LISTNEST_OFF` | 56 | nested `<ul>`/`<ol>` from `w:ilvl` (flattens to one level) |
| `CSRELABEL_OFF` | 57 | "CS:" prefix → every retained note back to "RED FLAG:" |
| `ACTINT_OFF` | 58 (ext. 64) | activity `.interactive` post-pass (inner-placeholder-owned interactive widget → plain activity); round 64 extended it — multi-type marker scan + the dropDown/typing/selectionBox/clickingOrder/crossword task types — so this toggle reverts the WHOLE post-pass incl. round 64 |
| `FACETABLE_OFF` | 59 | orphan free-body `[back]` table → flipCard placeholder capture (reverts to the raw leaking `<table>`) |

ONE rule (a row is a FRONT then a BACK) covering every measured dialect: the front rides the opener (`[hint slider 1] The whistle blew…`), the back is a `[back]`/`[body]` tag (OSAI401/501's `[back] [body]`), the back is a LABELLED red span (`reveals 🡪 …`), the halves are named in the bracket (`[hint slider front]`/`[hint slider back]`, SCPH301), the front is a black bullet (`• **A bit**`). `min_rows` is 1 because **44.7% of the gold's 161 sliders are single-row**. | `SBRICH_OFF` | 276 | **SPEECHBUBBLE — BUILD EVERY VARIATION** (Chris, the interactive-coverage chain round 1 of 8; **NO regeneration — not requested; the r275 baselines still describe the corpus**). Reverts the RICH GENERAL COMPOSER, so a speech bubble the writer did not lay out as exactly one 1x2 table goes back to the hand-off box. MEASURED with a probe that rewrites every `return null` in the speechBubble region to a recorder (`outputs/_measure_r276_speechbubble.cjs`), so the SHIPPED builder names the guard that declined each bundle: **100% of the 258 declines were decided in the narrow 1x2-table branch**, on guards about the writer's LAYOUT not the widget — 72 `tables.length!==1` (69 with no table at all), 67 `bundle.modifier` (ANY modifier: "left", "orange", "insert bookworm on right hand side"), 50 `rows.length!==1`, 38 `row.length!==2` (27 of them ONE cell); 87% of the class. The shape census could not have found this — its two largest DECLINING families are also its two largest BUILDING families. FULL FUNNEL (`_measure_r276_funnel.cjs`, live extractor): 1,005 bubble bracket spans / 60 modules → 992 (98.7%) resolve → 585 captured → 505 built; alias gap = `hoverbubble`×6 (a HOVER widget, deliberately not re-pointed), `speeechbubble`×2, `rhs thoughtbubble`×1. ON (default): `#speechBubbleRich` runs **LAST**, only where conversation / no-table-avatar / text-only / 1x2-table all returned null — **strictly additive, so the 327 pre-round builds are byte-identical BY CONSTRUCTION** (loosening the existing guards would have risked them; a fallback cannot). It flattens members AND every table cell into an ordered stream of PARTS (bubble / image / head / text / note) and emits ONE `row speechBubble` per bubble — avatar form or text-only form, reusing the r246/r247 templates because the gold measurement (`_measure_r276_goldforms.py`, **1218 rows / 143 modules**) says those ARE the plurality: one bubble per row **80.4%** (an r182 SOLIDIFY — so a multi-bubble group emits STACKED rows, never one packed row), avatar rows `layout=speech` **97.6%** + bubble-RIGHT **58.6%** vs left 12.4%, image-less rows `col-12` + `bubble-basic no-hover bubble-top`. Table cells GROUP by the axis yielding ≤1 bubble per group (rows → columns → cell-by-cell; the column axis is TEFUN03's 2x3 image-row-above-caption-row grid). `plain_cell_bubbles`: a bare table under a bubble invocation becomes one bubble per cell (CEDR501 "[Put the three quotations each into a speech bubble]" + a 1x3 table; OSBY301 4x2) — gated on the opener being the invocation AND every cell plain text, so a captured DATA table still declines. Members classify by RESOLVED TAG first (`tag_roles`) — the spelling is unreliable ("[Insert image of a robot]" matches no `^image`; 11 declines were that) — and `image_re` is UNANCHORED (an anchored one sent OSBY301's `[flipped image]` into decline_tags via its "flip" prefix). `image_slug_fallback`: a non-iStock image takes the r126 rotating-banner URL-slug placeholder name instead of blocking the widget (a VIDEO url is never an avatar and still declines). An EMPTY invocation no longer bails the widget (BLLR201's "[Insert bookworm … + audiovisual item 5]" beside the real bubble) — its text surfaces as a red Writers Note, as do asset requests and writer instructions (the r242/r214 rule), only on a successful build. NEVER HALF-BUILDS: `decline_tags` (an [Activity] opener, another widget's invocation, a [button]/[video]/[link] with its own content), a nested widget, a captured table with neither bubble nor image (over-capture), an un-nameable image, a bubble with no text. **RESULT: speechBubble 327→505 of 585 = 55.9%→86.3%; overall interactive coverage 23.4%→26.9%; NEW BUILDS 178 / LOST 0 in EVERY type; 63 modules / 112 pages** (`outputs/_r276_affected.txt`). PROOF: the full 16-shard census under `SBRICH_OFF=1` **reproduces the r275 published census EXACTLY** (23.4%, 327/585, all 14 other types unchanged) — that is the same-tree baseline the +178/−0 is measured against; byte identity ONE STATE PER PROCESS (OFF == the shipped corpus 30/30 in-class pages; ON changes exactly 12 of those 30; out-of-class TRR203/ENGS302/HIS1006 **25/25 identical in the ON state**); speechBubble verifier **77 built / defect 0** + selftest GREEN; flipCard 33/div 0; accordion 17 panels all human-matching; carousel/clickDrop/tabs selftests GREEN; tags 9557/9557 REAL 0; entry-parity PASS; index-sync 33/28. ALL 80 remaining declines NAMED (changelog §5): 29 a FOREIGN element with its own content (**17 a `[button]` — the cross-cutting blocker the dashboard also lists against dragAndDrop 81 / clickDrop 51 / accordion 49; its own cross-type round**), 22 a captured non-bubble TABLE (capture boundary), 11 a bubble with NO TEXT AT ALL, 10 two images for one bubble, 4 a VIDEO url as the "image", 4 over-long. **TWO IMPLEMENTATION CATCHES, both caught by the VERIFIER:** removing a URL from `**<url>** / **text**` orphans the leading `**` and a naive edge-trim that strips `*` unbalances the REST of the line (`#sbRestText` drops the orphan WITH the " / " and never trims a marker that still has a partner); and the r104 STRAYLEAD class re-appeared as `<p>*</p>` + a leading `]`, now filtered on the composer's own lines. **VERIFIER RULE ADDED (general):** a built bubble whose every word is inside ONE human bubble is the human MERGING adjacent writer bubbles, not a garble — CONTAINMENT (≥4 tokens) is the discriminator, the r197 label-truncation precedent generalised; DETECTION unaffected. **STALE-ARTIFACT TRAP RECORDED:** the r275 census FILE reports 5 accordions as built that today's tree declines in BOTH toggle states — always diff against a same-tree `*_OFF` baseline, never a saved census from a previous round. Data `interactive_builders.speechBubble.rich` |

| `GOJOURNALDUP_OFF` | 273 | **THE DUPLICATE "Go to your journal" BUTTON** (Chris, the SCCH302-03 screenshot; **SCOPED REGENERATION of the 7 affected modules — scoped ship #1 since r266; every protected gate HELD, decomposition-proven**). Reverts the double-emit fix, restoring the SECOND identical `<h4 class="goJournal">` in a bare row underneath the closed activity box. ROOT CAUSE (a general mechanism gap): the r239 `absorb_into_activity` pulls a go-to-journal `[button]` sitting after a BUNDLE-OWNED activity's widget INSIDE the still-open box (`#goJournalTail`) and marks it `_consumed` — but the main loop has **no general `_consumed` guard for a TAG item**, only the positional `while (bodyItems[i + 1]?._consumed) i++` idiom, which cannot reach this button because the widget's own UNCONSUMED end tag (`[end click and drop]`) sits in between (the absorb walk steps over it by design). The loop therefore rendered the button again on its own turn via the `#element` button branch. ON (default): the absorb also marks the item `_goJournalAbsorbed` and the loop skips the ITEM outright, beside the `activity-super-content` / `activity-mode-merge` sentinels — skipping the item (not just its emission) is what stops an empty row/col being left behind, since the row opens further down. The r232 `#mtkQuizEmit` "emit exactly once, wherever the item is reached" discipline; contained BY CONSTRUCTION (only an item this absorb claimed is ever suppressed, and that absorb has already shipped the heading inside the box). MEASURED (`outputs/_detect_r273_gojournal.cjs`, the shipped guard over all 34 corpus modules carrying a goJournal heading both inside and outside an activity, 2 states): affected set **EXACTLY 10 pages / 7 modules** — CEDO102_0_0, ENGC102_3_0, ENGJ102_5_0, MXDI103_2_0/_5_0, MXFL103_1_5, SCCH301_3_0/_7_0, SCPH301_6_0/_8_0 — every diff a **pure deletion**; 3 further `_0_0` pages differ in BOTH states = pre-existing drift (no regen since r266), named and excluded. NOTE the naive "two headings adjacent" signature under-counts (another widget's hand-off box can render between the pair — SCCH301-07, SCPH301-08); use the guard-run detector. PROOF `outputs/_probe_r273_gojournal.cjs`, ONE PROCESS PER STATE, ALL CHECKS PASS both ways, re-run post-regen (fix-ON: SCCH302_3_0 ships exactly one heading + all 7 modules byte-identical to the regenerated corpus; toggle-OFF: the duplicate returns on EXACTLY the 10 named pages, one heading each; canaries BLL210/OSAH501/ENGS302/HIS1006 byte-identical in both states). The walk now crosses blank filler of ANY member type, stopping at the first member with a real primary tag (a URL belonging to another element can never be stolen) or at any line carrying prose of its own; reachable ONLY for a URL-less video, so **strictly additive**. Riders: a YouTube SHORTS url ships the r266 1x1 form and any other embeddable url the generic iframe, mirroring `MediaBuilder.media` instead of bailing on an unreadable host; and a slide line that is nothing but a bare video URL is DROPPED as the reference it is (**gold: 1,618 carousel captions, 0 bare-URL**; without it every unclaimed URL line rendered as a visible link paragraph). Data `carousel.rich_slides.video_url_recovery`. `CDIMAGE_OFF` — an `[image]` no longer bails a clickDrop: each item keeps an ORDERED part list and the image renders in the writer's own position as the standard Mode P/D asset (**gold: 939 of 2,877 clickDropContent blocks, 33%, across 235 modules carry an `<img>`**); an item with no image has exactly ONE body part holding the whole joined body, so every clickDrop that already built is byte-identical; an EMPTY media-list marker is SKIPPED (no asset to render — the XDLS903-906 golds ship none), a LEADING label-less `[click drop]` is the widget OPENER (the `[accordion]`/`[tabs]` convention), and text riding along with the URL is the item's prose CONTINUING after the image (HES1007's scenario panels), not a caption. Data `clickDrop.image_member`. PROOF: toggles-OFF reproduce the pre-round state on the 10 affected canaries (54 pages) while **6 out-of-class canaries (48 pages) are byte-identical in BOTH states**; per-toggle decomposition ONE STATE PER PROCESS (ACCTABLE 10 pages / CARVIDEO 5 / CDIMAGE 1); verifiers carousel **69 video slides, 0 mismatched ids**, accordion **48 panels all human-matching**, clickDrop **35 items, defect 0**, flipCard 33/div 0, speechBubble 47/defect 0, tabs 59/div 0, all 5 selftests GREEN. **RECORDED, not shipped:** the BARE-OPENER accordion table dialect (105 bundles / ~50 shapes, panel headings frequently EDITORIAL and absent from the WT — ENFUN04's gold invents 'Step one/two/three') · the clickDrop capture class (XDLS903-906) · the `[embed story]` PDF family (58, guarded by `deny_extensions`) · a FRESHNESS-CHECK FALSE POSITIVE in BOTH `_coverage_dashboard.py` AND `_stalecheck.sh` — each treats every `data/*.json` as output-affecting, so rebuilding the DIAGNOSTIC `Module_Feature_Index.json` (engine-loaded = false; not in `_modules.json`'s data_map), which §13 requires after every regeneration, makes both declare the corpus/census stale when nothing that can change a conversion moved; this round was verified **0-stale immediately after the final regen** and `_content_manifest.py diff` reports **IDENTICAL, 0 pages differ** afterwards — narrow both scans to the data_map files |

## 12. The finalise ritual (every shipped round)

1. **Prepend** a dated round entry to `BUILD_CHANGELOG.md` (newest first): the WT→human→Claude
   evidence, the measured class size, the fix (code + data flag + env toggle), and the
   **PROTECTED gates** line showing each gate held-or-improved.
2. **Bump** `app/js/Config.js` `AppVersion` (e.g. `260616.36 → 260616.37`) and add a matching
   `// ROUND NN (260616.NN): …` comment above it.
3. Note the **data flag** + **env toggle** you added (keep §11 of this file current).
4. **Update this CLAUDE.md** if a convention, gate baseline, toggle, or recipe detail changed.
5. **DELETE THE SPENT KICKOFF FILE (Chris, 2026-07-10 — non-negotiable).** *(The 2026-07-31
   SCCH302-series suspension of this rule ended at round 243: the Dev-Feedback Round 6 session
   completed the full regeneration + gates and swept all five series kickoffs in one pass —
   the standing rule is BACK IN FORCE.)* If this round was driven
   by a handover/kickoff file (a root `NEW_SESSION_MESSAGE__*.md` or similar), delete that file as
   part of finalising — the handover has served its purpose once the round ships and the changelog
   entry exists. A kickoff whose round was DEFERRED or superseded is deleted by the session that
   records the deferral (the changelog/track entry is the durable record, not the kickoff). These
   files must never accumulate at the project root again — Chris has had to purge them twice.

## 13. Measurement-tools convention

Corpus-wide probes live in `outputs/` as `_measure_<class>.py` (or `_probe_*.cjs` for engine-level
bundle inspection). Use dynamic paths — `os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))` + `../../../01-…`
— **never hard-code a `/sessions/<id>/…` path** (those are per-session and go stale). Existing tools
to copy: `_measure_celltag.py`, `_measure_headinglevel.py`, `_measure_redflags.py`,
`_measure_multirow.py`, `_measure_layouttable.py`, `_measure_named*.py`, `_probe_parse.cjs`.

**`anchor_compare.py` — the DIAGNOSTIC safety net (round 144).** Not a protected gate; run after any
converter change: **`python3 anchor_compare.py --audit`** = whole-corpus **coverage** (0 silent drops,
per-template, non-zero exit only on a tool failure) + the **row.supervisor regression** (proves the
ancestor-class ladder diff catches a child→ancestor miss) + the **child→ancestor rule catalogue**
(`--rules` → `outputs/Child_Ancestor_Rules.{json,md}`: "ancestor E gets class/attr X ONLY when descendant
F present" with consistency/leak, per-template+series cross-tab, IMPLEMENTED-vs-OPEN by re-mining Claude).
Also `--coverage` / `--rules` / `--regress`. MUST resolve dirs via `_corpus.mdir` (a raw
`os.path.join(CLAUDE/HUMAN,code)` silently killed the WHOLE corpus after the round-128 nested move —
fixed round 144). Diagnostic-only → never moves a protected gate.

**`_mine_rendering_rules.py` — the GRANULARITY miner (round 161).** The rule catalogue the old miner
cannot see: FOUR shapes — added-token (now per-group), layout-form (col width-token swap, e.g. the
interactive-activity widening), style-transfer (WT `*italic*`/`**bold**` kept-vs-stripped by REGION
menu/body/activity/table/widget/acks) and composition (paired-vs-standalone, inner rows, sibling
order) — mined over all → template → subject → series × page role, reported at the coarsest passing
level with fragments. Chunked for the 45s wall: `--vocab` → `--scan H k 4` ×4 / `--scan C k 2` ×2 →
`--merge` → `outputs/Rendering_Rules_Catalogue.{json,md}` (+ ranked OPEN backlog). Its `--selftest`
must rediscover the FOUR known-answers (menu italic r145 / BLL pairing + inner row r160 / col-width
decomposition / row.supervisor r143.2) AND re-find all 30 legacy rules — `anchor_compare --audit`
runs it automatically when the catalogue exists (a FAIL fails the audit); `anchor_compare --rules2`
re-merges. The legacy `Child_Ancestor_Rules.{json,md}` stays produced unchanged alongside.

**Refactor-round tooling (round 149, ENGINE SPLIT track).** GOLDEN MANIFEST: `find
../../../01-Claude_Modules_ -name '*.html' -type f | sort | xargs md5sum >
outputs/_golden_manifest.txt` — a refactor round ships ONLY on an EMPTY diff after a full regen
(byte-identity, stronger than hold-or-improve). Gate-baseline record: `outputs/_phase0_gate_baseline.txt`.
Loader-migration inventory (which harnesses use `_engine_load.cjs`, which stay hand-listed until
touched): `outputs/_engine_load_migration.md`. Batch-plan weights for slow modules:
`reference/tests/_module_weights.json` (TRR1xx very_heavy; HPFUN/PES/SSFUN/TEFUN/XLP/XMES/XTAS/XWHA/
ENGC/HIS heavy — learned round 149).

**Scoped-ship tooling (round 175 — §10a).** `scoped_ship.sh` (the checkpoint), `_content_manifest.py`
(md5-per-page byte-diff + content-hash 0-stale — `snapshot`/`diff`/`changed`/`fresh`; the permanent
`outputs/_content_manifest.txt` IS the last-shipped state, refreshed by every ship), `_scoped_spotcheck.py`
(completeness `plan`/`verify`), `_ship_ledger.py` (full-ship cadence), `_fastloop_diff.py --commit` (the
scoped baseline refresh), `regen_affected.cjs` v2 (`--wt-regex` / `--from-list`). A round's affected-set
detector is a per-class probe over ALL 390 WTs — copy `outputs/_detect_bracketfix.cjs` (runs the shipped
guard, keeps where it fires = a complete superset). NEVER scope the detector to `compare_set` (197) — it
misses out-of-set modules like ENGC101 (round-174 lesson).

**Granular-registry + vetting + subject-parameters tooling (round 178 — the corpus-verification machinery).**
All in `reference/tests/`, each with a `--selftest`:
- **`scaffold_sig.py`** — the shared single source of truth: `granular_sig(el)` (the full row/col-WIDTH/
  wrapper ancestor ladder), `group_keys(code)` (series→phase→prefix→subject→corpus), the reliability
  floors (`MIN_MODULES` 5, `MAJ_SHARE` 0.60, `STRONG_SHARE` 0.80), `nav_tab_labels(raw)`.
- **`build_granular_registry.py`** — builds `data/Granular_Scaffold_Registry.json` **and (round 179)
  `data/Module_Structure_Index.json`** (per-module built ladder for every element, retrievable — the
  basis for the sibling-precedence lookup) from the human gold. Chunk-friendly for the 45 s wall —
  **ROUND 253 RECIPE (the 454-dir corpus outgrew the old 12-way slice): run
  `outputs/_r253_shard_driver.sh` repeatedly until it prints ALL_SHARDS_PRESENT (48-way shards,
  resumable — finished shards are skipped), then `--merge` → `--selftest`.** `--query "menu|@h5"`
  inspects a key. Keep ONE shard-N per build (mixing N double-counts). Concurrency does NOT help
  (the sandbox is single-core) and a detached background job does not survive the call boundary.
- **`granular_consensus.py`** — the reader anchor_compare + vetting consume. **The AUTHORITY MODEL,
  round 182 (Chris): `resolve(code, key)` walks the 6-LEVEL cascade** (doc14 → series+template →
  subject+phase+template → subject+template → subject → corpus), STOPS at the first level that SOLIDLY
  confirms a value (consolidation levels `decision:"solidify"` — assert only when share ≥ solidify_share
  AND not a tie, else OMIT + defer up), CLIMBS A RUNG on an omitted level,
  ESCALATES only when none solidifies + doc-14 silent — reading `data/Precedence_Cascade.json` (order/floors) +
  `Module_Structure_Index.json` (`module_meta` + `modules`). `adjudicate()` now calls `resolve` (its
  round-179 3-level body kept verbatim as `_adjudicate_legacy` for `CASCADE6_OFF`). `cascade_selftest()`
  proves each of L1–L7 is a real decider (+ doc-14 override + the tie-defers-up rule + escalate), FAILS if
  any level is unreachable (`--cascade-selftest`). `predecessors()`/`precedent()`/`scaffold_verdict()`/`finest_group()` are retained
  (used by the legacy path + subject_parameters). `tab_verdict` still uses the corpus positional norm
  (per-series tab precedent is a follow-up).
- **`PrecedenceResolver.js`** (app/js — the ENGINE mirror) + **`_verify_precedence_parity.cjs`** +
  **`_parity_compare.py`** — the engine-side `resolve` reading the same 3 data files via DataService; the
  parity harness proves JS↔Python resolve IDENTICAL (1504/1504 pairs). `ContentConverter.inheritMenu`
  consults it behind `Precedence_Cascade.engine_inherit.enabled` (INERT default → byte-identical; env
  `INHERIT_ON`/`INHERIT_OFF`). Sizing: **`outputs/_measure_cascade.py`** (which level decides, corpus-wide;
  finds the per-level selftest fixtures + the escalate rate) and **`_measure_cascade_engine.py`** (the E×C×H
  contingency — engine vs cascade vs human — for the engine-wiring net: menu|@h5 = +25).
- **`vet_issue.py`** — WS3 front door: `--code X --page N --desc "…"`, or bare (scan `ZZ-Screenshot-To-Fix/
  NEW/` + top-level pngs) → verdict card + `_Vetting_Ledger.{md,json}`; `--actioned` moves NEW→ACTIONED.
- **`subject_parameters.py`** — WS4 doc-14 mechanism over `data/Subject_Global_Parameters.json`:
  `--demo CODE`, `--demo-active` (one per active family). `master_enabled:false` = inert (no output effect).
The MEASUREMENT MANDATE extends here: a submitted issue is VETTED against the registry (`ONE-OFF HUMAN
OUTLIER` = the human page deviates from the corpus majority at the finest reliable grouping → do NOT change
the converter) BEFORE any converter change. This is the standing front door for the debugging period.


**Feature index + selective regeneration (round 271 — Chris: the corpus is slow to rebuild).**
`data/Module_Feature_Index.json` labels all 454 modules with the features they exhibit on BOTH
sides — the WT tags the normaliser resolves (+ raw bracket spellings), the widget types those
fold to, and the structural features present in the converted HTML. Built by
**`reference/tests/build_feature_index.cjs`** (`--shard K 16` ×16 → `--merge`; `--rehtml` refreshes
the OUTPUT side alone after a regeneration, no .docx re-extraction; `--selftest`). Consumed by
**`reference/tests/regen_scope.cjs`** — `--feature carousel [--list|--plan]` resolves a change to
its module list and prints a batch plan that feeds the §10a scoped ship unchanged; `--features`
lists what can be scoped and the saving; `--explain CODE` shows one module's features;
`--verify <changed.txt>` proves after a regen that every truly-changed module was inside the scope.
**Safety: over-select, never under-select** — a feature matches on the TAG or the HTML (a module
whose new branch might now fire is included even though its output shows nothing today);
corpus-wide classes (skeleton/footer/menu/acks/tag normalisation/pagination/formatter/naming/notes)
are REFUSED with a pointer to the full ship; an unknown feature is REFUSED rather than resolving to
an empty scope; and a STALE index (older than the newest WT or engine file) is FATAL, because
under-selection is the one failure mode that silently ships a stale corpus. **REBUILD THE INDEX
after every corpus intake, lexicon change or regeneration.** Savings: glossary 99% of the corpus
skipped, shapeHover 96%, tabs 83%, selfCheck 59%, accordion 53%, carousel 25%.

**THE STANDING DISCREPANCY DASHBOARD (2026-08-06, Chris).** `outputs/_coverage_dashboard.py` →
`outputs/COVERAGE_DASHBOARD.md` (+ `.json`): every remaining discrepancy class corpus-wide,
**ranked by how many MODULES it costs**, so the round queue is chosen by impact rather than by
whichever screenshot arrived. Diagnostic only — reads artifacts other tools already produce
(the r271 census shards · the newest `_r*_sk_final.json` · `body_compare.json` ·
`_structural_defect_audit.py --json` · `Module_Feature_Index.json`), writes nothing to the
corpus, moves no gate. `--refresh` re-runs the cheap defect audit; `--selftest` proves the
parsers still bind; `--selftest --crosscheck` re-runs `body_compare.py` and proves the dashboard
reproduces it exactly.

- **It promotes INTERACTIVE COVERAGE to a headline number beside the scaffold gate** (currently
  **2,232 / 5,122 = 43.6%** at round 284 — the chain REGENERATED into the corpus; the 14-build gap to
  round 283's 2,246 is the NAMED r284 data-recovery residual, changelog §3 — from 42.0% at r282, 38.5% at r281, 38.2% at r280, 36.8% at r279, 30.5% at r278, 28.1% at r277, 26.9% at r276, 23.4% at r275 and 21.4% at r274). §9's skeleton gate deliberately EXCLUDES widget internals, so it
  can read "held" while the visible product does not improve — scaffold moved 49.55 → 49.86%
  across rounds 245–275 while ~3,714 tagged widgets still ship as hand-off boxes. Judge a
  widget-building round on coverage; judge a structural round on the skeleton (§6 mandate
  unchanged).
- **A DASHBOARD MUST REUSE A GATE'S OWN PREDICATE, never a looser proxy.** The first cut tested
  `over_capture > 0` and reported 1,488 pages against `body_compare.py`'s real 71, putting a
  phantom class at #1. The body rows now copy the gate's compound flags verbatim (over ≥0.40 AND
  chars >400 AND lost_blocks ≥3 · runaway multi_type ≥4 · empty truthy) — crosscheck-verified
  71/5/193/267. Keep them in lockstep if `body_compare.py` changes.
- **Census staleness is FATAL by default** (`--allow-stale` overrides): a census older than the
  newest `app/js`/`data` change UNDER-reports coverage and would aim a round at an already-fixed
  class. Rebuild first (16 shards, ~10s each) after ANY builder change.
- **The queue's top rows are the un-built widget types** (dragAndDrop 292 modules · carousel 254 ·
  flipCard 191 · accordion 172 · clickDrop 144), and §4 of the report names the CROSS-CUTTING
  BLOCKERS that gate them — accordion refuses a captured TABLE 190/0, carousel `embed` 60/0 and
  `image` 60/0, clickDrop `image` 179/5, accordion `button+txt` 49/0 — plus the four types with
  NO builder case at all (hint / infoTrigger / selfCheck / slider, 300 widgets). Take a BLOCKER,
  not a shape: the declines spread over hundreds of distinct authoring shapes per type, so
  one-dialect-per-round cannot close the class.

**Dropdown tooling + a shard trap (round 287).** `outputs/_measure_r287_ddauthor.cjs` is the
RED-PRESERVING authoring dump — the first r287 probe recorded only `memberItems` and ran the
text through a whitespace clean, losing the two things a dropdown builder needs (the opener,
which is the only reliable dropbox-vs-dropdown discriminator, and WHICH WORDS WERE RED, which
is the answer). `_measure_r287_ddsize.py` sizes each dialect before coding;
`_measure_r287_ddgold.py` measures the human's output convention body-scoped;
`_probe_r287_dropdown.cjs` (build + `--hashes`, one toggle state per process) and
`_probe_r287_leaks.cjs` (the defect audit's own predicate, per module) are the proof pair.
**THE SHARD TRAP, refined:** the first dump merged 15 of 16 shards silently. It was NOT the
round-279 parallel-write loss — **shard 11 is simply a slow shard that exceeds the 40s wall
when run alongside three others and completes alone.** A merge must say so loudly (this one
now prints `!! SHARDS MISSING`), and a missing shard under-reports a population by ~8%.
**DENOMINATORS DIFFER BETWEEN TOOLS:** the all-type decline recorder counts every captured
bundle (7602) and reports 29.7% coverage; the standing dashboard's denominator is narrower
and reports ~43%. Quote one tool's before/after, never a figure from each.

**Builder-coverage census (round 271).** `outputs/_measure_r271_variations.cjs` walks every module,
records an authoring-SHAPE signature for every captured non-complex bundle and whether the shipped
builder built it (`--merge` = the coverage table, `--report <type>` = declining shapes);
`outputs/_r271_cluster.py` clusters those signatures into DIALECTS. Current coverage **43.9%**
(2246 built / 2876 declined, round 283 — the clickDrop general composer; the round also
CORRECTED the dashboard's own headline clickDrop row, whose named `[image]` blocker was
fixed at round 275 and now decides only 23 declines).
Older r282 text: **42.0%** (2152 built / 2970 declined, round 282 — the flipCard general composer; the round again asked the
registry question FIRST and found flipCard has no who-you-are gate at all, so the blocker was
entirely the table readings and the demand for an image on every card).
Older r281 text: **38.5%** (1973 built / 3149 declined — the tabs general composer; the round PROVED,
by neutralising every gold-choice registry gate, that the registry gains exactly ONE build and is
therefore not the blocker for tabs).
Older r280 text: **38.2%** (1956 built / 3166 declined — the modal's general trigger + TKmodal
set fallback).
Older r279 text: **36.8%** (1883 built / 3239 declined — the carousel table-slide fallback + the
table section break).
Older r278 text: **30.5%** (1552 built / 3542 declined — the accordion panel-delimiter vocabulary).
Older r277 text: **28.1%** (1426 built / 3656 declined — the hint element + the labelled-pair slider).
Older r276 text: **26.9%** (1368 built / 3714 declined — speechBubble's rich composer). Older r275 text: **23.4%**
(1190 built / 3892 declined, round 275 — the three builder blockers). The declines are a LONG TAIL of shapes (610 carousels across 462
signatures), so the lever is NOT more per-shape branches — it is the rich fallbacks' member
VOCABULARY (`[button]` 73/0 in accordion, `[embed]` 67/0 in carousel, `[data marker]` 0 everywhere,
`[image]` 105/7 in accordion) and their refusal of a captured TABLE (accordion 190/0). Sized as the
next rounds.

## 14. Current state snapshot (keep in sync with the changelog)

- **Build:** `260618.63` (round 293 — **TABS: build every variation** (Chris — the interactive-coverage chain, round 5 of 8: accordion ✅ → flipCard ✅ → clickDrop ✅ → modal ✅ → tabs ✅ → dropDown → carousel → speechBubble; **SCOPED regeneration of the 6 affected modules, authorised in the request — NOT a full rebuild; the §9 baselines still describe the round-288/289/290/291/292 state except for the 9 named pages**). Tabs are a row of clickable headings with a panel behind each. 79 of the 113 the writers tagged were hand-off boxes — the smallest population in the set and the worst covered. **They now build 38 of 113, up from 34, with nothing lost in any other widget type.** The count is the least interesting thing about the round. **Round 292 recorded a rule — a brief's stated reasons are trustworthy exactly when its widget's composer is last in the dispatch — and it predicted this round precisely.** Tabs has six readings, five of which have fallen through to a general composer since round 281, so the brief's technical notes point at code that decides nothing: **its seven reasons collapse to three terminal causes, all in one place**, and its reasons B and F, seventeen activities between them, name a builder whose refusal has not decided anything for twelve rounds. **Its ranking is upside down for the fourth round running, and this time two of its entries are worth less than nothing.** Its top-ranked change — strip a `Tab N:` prefix from a table header — reaches **exactly one** activity, not the "8+ of 39" claimed, and that one is blocked by two other things first. Its fifth — allow `[image]` inside a pane — reaches **zero**: `[image]` blocks nothing, and all twelve of those declines are another widget's marker swept in by the capture. Its fourth is not a builder change at all: the writer declares five tab names and the material is in the next section, and the human developer built no tabs there either. Against that, five rules landed on the human's own labels exactly — a `[Tab N]` marker riding on another tag (**XGF9006 3/3, XGF9003 3/3**), a wholly-red row read as either a role row or the labels themselves (**OSAI201 4/4, TEDC401 4/4** — the brief's own headline example), content around the table read as the widget's lead and tail rather than a disqualifier, the `Tab N` prefix, and the opener repeating with its label (**PHE1007 3/3**). A sixth is shipped **and reported as reaching zero**: it runs on seven activities, builds none of them, and saying otherwise would be false. **THREE BUGS THE PROOF CAUGHT BEFORE THEY SHIPPED** — `[Tab 1 - H3]` read as a name, shipping three tabs all called "H3" over a build that already matched its gold (**invisible to the build census; caught only by hashing every page of every tabs module in both states**); the prefix strip quietly editing pane prose as well as labels; and restored content landing above the tabs when the writer put it after. **And two of my own checks were wrong before any code was**, for the third round running. GATES: pages ≥50%, ≥75%, compare-structure exact, body, clean, leak and tags **all EXACT**; the two movers named and decomposed page by page — over nine pages the scaffold falls 3.25 pp while **RAW rises 24.19 pp**, and the only page down on both is one where content that was being **silently dropped** is now on the page. **A FINDING WORTH CHRIS'S ATTENTION:** three of the nine changed pages are **not this round's at all** — they are rounds 289's and 290's pending work, proven by toggle, because those rounds checked byte-identity on a sample of their widget's modules rather than the whole population. The residue is swept here, and **a full `REGENERATE CORPUS` would sweep any more of the same kind**. Env `TABMARKERBRACKET_OFF` / `TABMARKERROW_OFF` / `TABLEADIN_OFF` / `TABPREFIX_OFF` / `TABOPENERLABEL_OFF` / `TABCELLTAGS_OFF`. PRIOR round below.)

- **Build:** `260618.62` (round 292 — **THE POP-OUT (MODAL): build every variation** (Chris — the interactive-coverage chain, round 4 of 8: accordion ✅ → flipCard ✅ → clickDrop ✅ → modal ✅ → tabs → dropDown → carousel → speechBubble; **SCOPED regeneration of the 9 affected modules, authorised in the request — NOT a full rebuild; the §9 baselines still describe the round-288/289/290/291 state except for the 10 named pages**). A pop-out is the window that opens when a learner clicks something. 219 of the 380 the writers tagged were hand-off boxes — the most concentrated type in the set. **They now build 195 of 380, up from 161, with nothing lost in any other widget type.** Two things matter more than the count. **This is the first brief in the chain whose reason counts are ACCURATE**, and the reason is structural rather than lucky: the modal composer is already tried LAST and keeps its own member walk, so its refusals are genuinely terminal — which tells the next rounds exactly when to trust a brief and when not to (tabs, round 5, has five builders and is not in that position). **But its ranking is still upside down in both directions.** Its top-ranked change — follow `[Insert media item N]` to a picture — is real and correct, and reaches **15 activities in three modules, not "most of the 80"**; while the thing actually blocking those modules goes unmentioned: a leading numberless marker (the writer setting the widget up) and a `[Close Modal]` were each opening a trigger-less pop-out that swallowed the tile table and sank the whole widget. Its "51 pieces that cannot be placed" is the same appear-vs-block artefact rounds 289–291 each found, in a third form — **`[button]` and `[video]` block nothing**, and the true joint-largest lever is **26 stray markers belonging to a neighbouring activity**, every one of which sits at the very end of its bundle carrying no text at all, so dropping them is measurably lossless rather than a judgement call. And **25 of its 46 "a sentence, not a button label" carry no text whatsoever**. Six things shipped, each reversible on its own: the stray marker skipped like a `[button]`; a closer is not a pop-out; a leading text-less marker is the widget's opener; the Media-List tile strip, which lands on **EXPFUN04's gold's own three filenames in the gold's own column class** and is all-or-nothing so a row is never half pictures and half words; the bold opening phrase as the name; and `[external link]` as content. **The modal was also the only widget in this chain with no verifier at all** — there is one now, in the gate suite, selftest GREEN, and **it caught a bug in itself before it caught anything else** (Mode-P ships every picture twice, once inside an HTML comment, so every trigger counted double). PROOF: the toggles-OFF census reproduces round 291 exactly; **the affected set was derived from BYTES rather than builds** — three of the changes sit in the member walk that also runs for bundles which already build, so a module could have changed bytes without changing its count, and hashing all 101 modal-carrying modules in both states found exactly the same 10 pages / 9 modules the census did; byte identity one state per process, with six out-of-class canaries identical in both; the leak guard per-module identical over all nine; a quality scan of 167 triggers flagging none; and the verifier reporting **defect 0**.
